# ss_manifest.txt -- every static of the save-state owning files, classified.
# Checked by external/ps3recomp/tools/ss_manifest_check.py (run it from the project root):
#   py external/ps3recomp/tools/ss_manifest_check.py [--map build/infamous2.map] [FILE...]
# With no FILE it checks every file named below; an unclassified static fails it.
#
#   <path>:<symbol> <CLASS> # <reason>
#
# path    from the project root, '/' separators
# symbol  a file-scope name, or <function>.<name> for a function-local static
# CLASS   SAVE   captured in a section (runtime/savestate/ss_state.h), restored on a load
#         RESET  back to its boot value, or rebuilt, on a load (M4c's teardown / recreate)
#         KEEP   host state a load keeps as it is: devices, handles, locks, caches, scratch
#         DIAG   diagnostics or statistics only: no behaviour depends on it
#         BOOT   set at startup (configuration, registration) and never after
# Read-only objects (static const tables, const char* const) need no line.
# Add your file's statics in one block per file, in source order.
# Coverage (M4b): every .c / .cpp of external/ps3recomp/{runtime,libs,third_party} and src/ the game links
# and that has a static is named here (a header's statics are its includers', through --map);
# generated/ holds only const tables. A new source file with state needs its block.

# ---- runtime/savestate/ss_core.c: the stop protocol and the coordinator -------------
# Nothing here is guest state: the engine's own machinery. A load runs inside a
# stop and releases it itself, so the protocol's state is KEEP; what belongs to
# the discarded threads is RESET with them.
external/ps3recomp/runtime/savestate/ss_core.c:g_ss_armed BOOT # armed at startup (SAVESTATES=0: never)
external/ps3recomp/runtime/savestate/ss_core.c:g_ss_req KEEP # the PPU stop gate: a load runs inside a stop and releases it
external/ps3recomp/runtime/savestate/ss_core.c:g_ss_spu_req KEEP # the SPU park gate, as g_ss_req
external/ps3recomp/runtime/savestate/ss_core.c:g_ss_mix_req KEEP # the mixer park gate, as g_ss_req

# environment switches are BOOT, its log limits DIAG, host frame pacing KEEP.

# ==== files: FSFD, the open files and directories of the three fd tables (M4b) ======
# runtime/syscalls/fsfd_ss.inc. sys_fs.c owns the section (check, capture, record
# layout); ppu_fs.cpp and cellFs.c each add a part that reads its table. An open slot
# is SAVE: M4c's teardown closes its host object (FILE*, DIR*, find handle) and its
# restore reopens it at the same fd of the same table from the record (guest path
# resolved again, sought to pos). Roots and environment switches are BOOT.

# ---- kernel group (M4b): PPUT / KOBJ / KMEM owners --------------------------------
# The kernel objects (KOBJ), the allocators (KMEM) and the PPU threads (PPUT);
# M4c's restore of each is described where the section is (ss_state.h API).

# sys_ppu_thread.c: the PPU thread table -- PPUT (this file). Host handles of the
# saved threads are RESET: M4c recreates every thread (main is reused).

external/ps3recomp/runtime/syscalls/sys_ppu_thread.c:s_ss_exiting RESET # thread-local: exiting (a transition: no stop is accepted while it is set)
external/ps3recomp/runtime/syscalls/sys_ppu_thread.c:g_vm_stack_alloc SAVE # KMEM stacks (ppu_sysprx.cpp writes it): the guest stack bump; each stack's guard page sits below it
external/ps3recomp/runtime/syscalls/sys_ppu_thread.c:g_ppu_thread_entry_trampoline BOOT # the scaffold's entry trampoline (ppu_loader.cpp, startup)
external/ps3recomp/runtime/syscalls/sys_ppu_thread.c:g_ppu_thread_root_reset BOOT # the scaffold's root-reset hook (ppu_loader.cpp, startup)
external/ps3recomp/runtime/syscalls/sys_ppu_thread.c:g_ppu_main_ctx KEEP # main's live context (ppu_run's local; a host pointer, never saved -- PPUT saves the context); main is reused by a load

# ---- src/main.cpp ----
# The frame clock and the host glue (main.cpp). Nothing here is guest state: the guest's
# flip count is cellGcm's (GCM_). After a load M4c resyncs the clock (s_next_tick = now,
# s_last_flip = the restored flip count: design 6.2, ss_host_ops).

# ---- src/if2_menu.cpp ----
# Native entries in the game's menus (NATIVE_MENU; N1: Quit Game, N2: PC Settings in the pause menu). Everything
# they add lives in guest memory (GMEM) -- our strings in the game's dictionary, the entries' labels and links, the
# settings screen's rows and its state block (anchored at string id 0xF1FFF) -- and is re-checked from guest memory
# every frame: the caches below are keyed on guest objects and contents (a load that puts back another menu, other
# strings or another screen state is re-read on the next frame), or are the settings store's host state (KEEP).
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_on BOOT # NATIVE_MENU, read once at startup
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_diag BOOT # MENU_DIAG, read once at startup
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_skip BOOT # MENU_TEST_SKIP, read once at startup
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_menu KEEP # the last menu classified (re-validated against guest memory every frame)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_menu_sig KEEP # its action list's signature then (a change re-classifies)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_menu_kind KEEP # pause menu or not (re-derived with the signature)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_pc KEEP # our PC Settings entry in that pause menu: item, text boxes, label (re-derived with the signature)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_quit KEEP # our Quit Game entry in that pause menu (re-derived with the signature)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_sel_mask KEEP # that menu's Select button mask (re-derived with the signature)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_said DIAG # log limiter
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_sel_prev KEEP # Select held on our entry last frame (an edge latch, re-read from the game's joystick every frame)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_retry KEEP # frames until a pause menu we could not add to is tried again (a throttle)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_diag_focus DIAG # MENU_DIAG: the focus last logged
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_str KEEP # text -> string id cache of our dictionary records (each use re-checks the record in guest memory)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_str_next KEEP # the next string id to try (an id a loaded state uses is skipped)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_str_scanned KEEP # our records a loaded state brought were read once (a miss later only makes a new one)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_model KEEP # the overlay's menu model, this front end's instance (tabs / rows rebuilt from the store on every open)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_model_ready KEEP # the model was initialised
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_pages KEEP # the screen's categories, built from the store's table (rebuilt on open and when the table grows)
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_pages_rows KEEP # opt_count() when they were built
src/if2_menu.cpp:g_gen_seen KEEP # the store's generation the rows were synced to (the store is host state, not saved)

# sys_vm: unused by this title -- KOBJ's check refuses while a mapping is live
# (sys_vm_ss_live) and records the count (asserted_empty.vm_maps, 0). Unmapping
